## Review — 2026-10-26

H04 completed **PASS (HW OBSERVED, OFF-only)** on the current runner. The panel clamp readbacks were CH1 `2.00 V` from a `2.01 V` request, CH2 `3.00 V` from `3.01 V`, and CH3 `4.00 V` from `4.01 V`; all three output readbacks were OFF and each associated `SYST:ERR?` response was `0, "No error"`. The library rejected each above-profile voltage/current request before sending SCPI. The `unrestricted()` low-setpoint check, active-profile immutability, `power_only` API restrictions, and CH1 voltage-limit / CH3 current-setpoint tamper detection passed. The report contains 586 `SYST:ERR?` replies; every reply was `0, "No error"`.

No output was energized and no load was connected. The operator confirmed all three PSU channels OFF and the load disconnected after the runner completed. The PSU was accessed through CP2102 `/dev/ttyUSB3`, 9600 8N1. Firmware observations were `1.04-1.04` and `1.04-0.01`; menu Main/Aux remains 1.04/0.01. This does not qualify powered regulation or independent analog OVP. The earlier failed H04 attempt is preserved separately; its panel token was premature because Max Volt Set remained 4 V on all channels, and it is not counted as a pass.
